Carry the original Trace record into a face; share positions across indexed faces; return PointBody/CubeBody; generate a family by parameters; distinguish resolution, proportion and composition; end with a finite stopping rule and a composition of known bodies. 122 native assertions pass; ten faults are rejected. The 3 October starter-route descriptions below are historical, not the current host contract. Next boundary: Transformation inherits these classes; human learning and physical-headset comfort still need observation.

| Level | Read | Ask |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Whole project | [Brief](BRIEF.md), [25 sequence arcs](SEQUENCES.md) | What is Ada becoming? Which questions, bodies or capacities are missing? |
| Sequence | Its authored arc, short hall summaries and adjacent sequence boundaries | What arrives, develops and leaves? Why this order? What is supplied, repeated, deferred or unresolved? Preserve its particular critical approach. |
| Map | Full summary, review note, selected artifacts, intent/tutorial/critical/final texts | What exactly happens, what is taught, and why does this encounter matter here? |
| Evidence | Relevant source or saved observation, with version and scope | Which precise claim is supported, contradicted or still unknown? |

Use the [museum route](../../iterations/2026-10-04-museum-tutorial-storyboards/index.html) for hall/source links. Load detailed evidence selectively; retain this core and the current sequence's purpose during local work.

## Working loop

Whole → sequence → map → evidence → map → sequence → whole.

Before descending, keep a short position: **question; sequence/map; inherited decisions; why local work matters**. Before leaving, record: **finding or change; reason; status; source; affected neighbours; open question**. Update existing summaries and notes together when a teaching decision changes. Carry back only findings that affect the larger view.

A local discovery may change the sequence. A changed sequence may change the larger argument. Check both directions. Resume from the recorded question; do not reconstruct intent from the latest artifact alone.

Parallel readers can inspect different sequences against one saved state. Return claims and evidence, not automatic edits. A synthesis pass must inspect connections between their sequences. Shared assumptions and agreement are not independent verification.

Pilot: [4 October test](../../iterations/2026-10-04-context-roundtrip-test/index.html) source-checked thirteen frozen inputs; one fresh reader retained 8/8 required distinctions from 402 words. This tests one explicit handoff, not long-term reliability, runtime behavior or curriculum quality. Keep source hashes and claim scope beside compressed findings.

Judge fidelity, teaching progression and artistic force separately. Prefer "exact for this claim" and "sufficient for this encounter" over a permanent "perfect" label. Review mature sequences lightly unless evidence gives a reason to reopen them. A text/artifact gap may call for restoring an encounter; it does not automatically justify cutting text.

## Forces — author redirects the route through the sphere, 6 October

Author wants the familiar point sphere to gain player abilities in steps: two held spheres enabling flight, launch-pad level, vortex, falling, more pads and shootable enemies. Proposed main-route reductions do not delete artifacts or commit map moves. Source distinctions: resolution catalyst already sends copied sphere forms; interactive_point_origin_force attracts rigid bodies but does not implement two-sphere flight. force_pad assigns launch velocity; jump_pad locks horizontal flight and snaps landing; force_vortex replaces velocity. Existing field_carry_provider is a possible single-owner XR locomotion seam. Existing Forces projectile combines increased damping with immediate velocity reduction; CatalystFoe personality changes are not momentum transfer. A new dynamic target/force receiver is needed for the proposed lesson. Preserve the two-text contract and test complete printed learner continuations in the museum. No runtime or canonical manuscript changes and no new Godot test in this proposal pass. Existing comics/native images explicitly labelled as prior evidence, not new renders. Author subsequently selected the flight control: aim down/back and hold triggers to thrust up/forward. Each sphere supplies the opposite of its aim; release/drop clears only that contribution, preserving velocity and gravity. This input choice is accepted; the broader map reordering remains a proposal. PLAN.md and encounters.json now record the choice and the illustrated report was rebuilt. Report's eight cards and local links validated; browser preview inspected.

## Forces — the unintended, 7 October

Author expands Forces from abilities to consequences: physics permits mechanically strange situations, comedy and spillover; an interface promising total control may erase the chance to stay with uncertainty. References: Chaplin/Modern Times, the Half-Life scientist, Keats negative capability, Nikita Diakur, Theo Triantafyllidis, Katie Torn. Made three original four-panel comics with the familiar student and black sphere: a spring helper whose rolling base complicates its task; three local agents making a blockage and moving their environment; consumer attachments changing the point's balance and contacts. Sources distinguish Diakur's simulated rigs, Triantafyllidis's Ritual inhabitants and Torn's assemblage practice; do not claim identical physics techniques. Keats is a literary reference applied interpretively, not an argument against causal investigation. Preserve distinction between emergent consequence, prescribed scene and numerical failure. ## Forces — precision, failure and the logical reveal, 7 October

Author broadens the catastrophe framing: Shorts invite a prediction and a physical reveal; precise success and understandable failure can both satisfy. Preserve this correction. Apparatus should pose a visible question (fit, land, balance, repeat, escape), permit ordinary success, make the decisive contact readable, and retain the aftermath for another use. ## 2026-10-08 — Fractal thinking: scale and return

User supplied 5afe47bd-8c89-48b9-971a-041bb3086ba0/Pasted text.txt; requested integration in Fractals final text and a spatial exploration. Core: finite encounters with a larger possibility space; walking within a world versus changing its generating rules; describe → draw → inhabit → revise. Changing scale changes what can be noticed and acted upon. Preserve differences between representations and ask what warrants carrying a local discovery into a hall, sequence or book. This names an artistic research method, not proof that nested scene nodes or a numerical recurrence are mathematical fractals. Selective translations need specific claims about what is omitted/added, not a universal information-loss theorem.

Edited only final.md in Fractal_Recursion (closing distinction), Fractal_RecursiveTrees (drawings/kept view/bodily return), Fractal_MengerSponge (hole versus route changes the question), Fractal_Synthesis (method and artistic judgment, plus HISTORY wording corrected for current latest-only Cantor default). Two fixed-scale MultiMesh projections (plan X/Z, side X/Y), current black over one kept rose endpoint snapshot; KEEP/CLEAR beside desk. Same source records; no change to generator, hand behavior or canopy ownership. Snapshot stores endpoints plus generation/ratio/fork/angle; rule settings alone cannot reconstruct a local hand turn. Keeps one local comparison in memory, not undo, cross-visit persistence, visitor tracking or aesthetic ranking. Noncolliding drawings, narrow physical support/shelf/base. Explicit invitation: keep a view, turn a branch, walk beneath it, describe again.
